! Title: Microsoft Blocker - Block all Microsoft Spying
! Description: Microsoft blocklist intended to block all connection to Microsoft excluding iso download, updates and security related calls.
! Homepage: https://codeberg.org/privacyfilters/Microsoft-Blocker

! Expires: 1 day
! Last modified: 27 October 2026 22:12:26 UTC
! Version: 2.16.0
! Syntax: Adblock
! Number of entries: 13217
!
! ! Custom Allow & Blocklist: windows update, defender, security, winget and vscode ! !
